Documentos · Design

Fonte: design/tripo/metamorfo-roteiro-tripo.md

Metamorfo: roteiro Tripo do nível 1 ao 150, mais a Fera de cristal (copia-e-cola)

Status: roteiro pronto para colar, 19/11. Nada foi gerado. Autor: Claude (art-director).
Modelo seguido: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (tabela de níveis, fluxo, frases de abertura e fecho), com os ajustes do criador de 19/11 (ver §0).
Fontes: design/gdd/metamorfo.md (§3.2 a 3.8), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.12, design/narrative/historias-das-classes-v2.md §8, design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md (§3, §9, §10, §11, §12), design/art/cores-classes-distancia-2026-11-19.md.
Entrada: só imagens nossas. Nunca usar asset da Asset Store ou do Fab como entrada.
Passada de personalidade 19/11 (régua nº 1: "os 12 personagens precisam ser INCRÍVEIS"):
- Identidade reescrita e embutida em todo corpo do 1 ao 150: silhueta em sino + cajado acima da cabeça, gorro de abas com sininho (peça fixa), sino-guia no peito como assinatura que cresce, braço de cristal.
- Contraste: lima viva dominante + creme #E8DFC8 como terceiro tom claro; turfa só secundária; lista Forbidden (druida, pele de bicho, Caçador, violeta na roupa, monocromia).
- Piloto: 1a = UM conceito por nó; 1b = pose T; corpo feminino com corte feminino real.
- 1-60 revisados: BULK do 26 (mulher: só nas peças), intermediários com ≥2 marcas novas de longe, sinos e rebanho de cristal só crescem, 60 com VIVID ACCENTS (ouro velho) + SHOWPIECE; armas 41-60 misturam A e B.
- 61-150: só o bloco de identidade foi trocado. Nada foi gerado.

Resumo

  1. Metamorfo (Ironroot, família Roupão): lima-líquen #A4C639 dominante + turfa #4A3B2A + cobre #B87333 (chocalhos); cristal violeta #8E5BD0 só de acento, só no braço esquerdo (no humano) e no corpo da Fera. Terceiro tom claro: creme #E8DFC8. Peça de cabeça fixa: gorro de feltro com abas e sininho (chifre de cristal na têmpora esquerda a partir da S4). Assinatura: sino-guia no peito, que cresce a cada série.
  2. Arma: cajado de pastor em gancho com chocalhos (cobre). Na Fera não há arma: as garras de cristal herdam o refino.
  3. Cadeia: 74 designs (8 únicos até o 37 + 33 níveis x 2 ramos do 41 ao 150). A = Guarda-Rebanho (lã, sinos, rebanho de cristal domado); B = Fera-Brava (cristal à mostra, garras, crista de lã).
  4. Níveis 1 a 37: 3 prompts por nível (HOMEM, MULHER, ARMA), todos partindo do corpo base e repetindo o bloco de IDENTIDADE. Níveis 41 a 150 (modelo de ramos): por nível, 1 ITEM (a armadura neutra, sozinha, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A e MULHER-B (o mesmo item interpretado por ramo) e 1 ARMA única. Nenhum depende de armadura anterior. Pose T e Multivista pelos moldes da §0. Pose T e Multivista pelos moldes da §0. Seção 7: a Fera de cristal (modelo próprio, quadrúpede, rig "Outro" no Tripo, animações próprias).
  5. Fim do arquivo: a Arte de apresentação (KEY ART e RETRATO, níveis 50 e 150) e os 2 prompts fixos de arte do item (fundo carvão). Só o nível 150 tem brilho sutil no traje; o cristal do Metamorfo (braço e Fera) pode ter um brilho interno leve, como no GDD.

0. Como usar

Fluxo de cada nível: REFERÊNCIA primeiro.

  1. HOMEM (nó "Gerar imagem"). MÉTODO NOVO (criador, 19/11): TODO nível (chave e intermediária) parte do CORPO BASE masculino em pose T, e o prompt manda ignorar a roupa atual ("IGNORE the current clothes and dress the character in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit: ...") descrevendo a roupa inteira do zero. A continuidade vem da paleta e dos temas do texto, não da imagem anterior.
  2. MULHER (nó "Gerar imagem"). Entrada = corpo base feminino em pose T, mais a referência HOMEM aprovada deste nível como guia de design. Mesmo método. ARMA: parte da arma do nível anterior (imagem da arma aprovada) e é redesenhada (cabeça e cabo novos).
  3. ARMA: da referência, "Extração de elementos" (só o cajado) e depois "Gerar imagem" para endireitar.
  4. ARMADURA (homem e mulher): template Pose T com o molde abaixo, depois Multivista. 3D só com o OK e o custo.

Molde POSE T (colar sobre cada referência aprovada): `` Same character and exact same outfit as the reference, staff removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms open, palms down, empty hands, plain grey background, neutral light, full body. ` Molde MULTIVISTA (sobre a pose T aprovada): ` Same character, front, back and side views of the exact same outfit in T-pose, empty hands, plain grey background, neutral light; the back shows the same pieces as the front. ``

Regras de design (as do Bárbaro, com os ajustes do criador de 19/11):

  • Paleta: lima #A4C639 em 45-55% (saturação >= 65%, valor alto, para não virar o verde do Caçador), creme #E8DFC8 como tom claro (golas, punhos, barras), turfa #4A3B2A secundária (nunca a maior mancha), cobre #B87333 acento. Violeta só no braço esquerdo (e nos chifres).
  • Silhueta Roupão: triângulo de base alta, corpo estreito, cajado em gancho acima da cabeça. Nunca: folhas, flores, pele de bicho, totem, metal pesado, chapéu pontudo.
  • CAPA ou MANTO só a partir da S5 (nível 41+); antes, no máximo uma gola pequena de feltro. Ornamento e crânios só da S4 em diante. S2 e S3 são novas e diferentes, mas sóbrias. A curva de poder sobe aos poucos.
  • Cada CHAVE muda MUITO (silhueta nova, botas novas, braçadeiras novas, arma redesenhada), legível a 10 m, e parte do CORPO BASE em pose T.
  • Cada INTERMEDIÁRIA SUBSTITUI >= 3 peças com desenho novo (túnica ou saia, botas, braçadeiras ou ombro; nunca só textura ou cor), com "REPLACE ... with ...", "NEW boots: ...", "NEW bracers: ...". Na ARMA, todo nível redesenha cabeça E cabo (mesma linha, mas visivelmente outro item).
  • Nada de ombro ou braço à mostra forçado, nem assimetria forçada. Do 41 em diante o traje cobre tudo, estilo Diablo IV. O braço esquerdo de cristal é a assinatura da classe: enfaixado na S1, antebraço de cristal à vista a partir da S3, sempre com o resto do braço coberto.
  • Brilho: traje fosco, sem emissão; só o cristal violeta pode ter um brilho interno muito leve (veias). O nível 150 tem o único brilho sutil de traje.
  • Feminino: mesmo design, paleta e gorro, com corte feminino real (corpete moldado ao busto, cintura bem marcada, barra abrindo do quadril como saia, bota alta; volume nas peças, nunca "o homem afinado"); sem pele mostrada além do antebraço de cristal.
  • Regra final do criador (19/11): todo nível troca peças E materiais. Túnica/barra do casaco, botas e braçadeiras têm desenho e material novos em todo nível (chave e intermediária); nunca repetir a mesma peça entre níveis. É a mesma classe em metamorfose.
  • Frente e costas com as mesmas peças. Nunca mais leve que a anterior. Prova: miniatura em cinza ao lado da anterior.
  • Ordem: todas as chaves partem do corpo base e rodam em paralelo; cada intermediária espera só a sua chave; A e B em paralelo do 41 em diante.

BLOCO DE IDENTIDADE DA CLASSE (vai em TODO prompt de HOMEM e MULHER, do 1 ao 150; passada de personalidade 19/11)

Traços desenháveis: silhueta = corpo estreito abrindo numa barra larga em sino (triângulo) + cajado em gancho acima da cabeça; peça de cabeça fixa = gorro de feltro lima com duas abas amarradas e um sininho no topo (madeira no 1-6, metal depois; chifre de cristal na têmpora esquerda da S4); detalhe-assinatura = o sino-guia grande no peito (madeira → cobre → prata → ferro-negro → cobalto → ouro velho; cresce a cada série) + o braço esquerdo de cristal. Contraste: lima viva dominante e mais clara, creme #E8DFC8 como terceiro tom claro (golas, punhos, barras), turfa só secundária (botas, cintos, correias, forros), cobre nos sinos. Forbidden: druida (folhas, cipó, galhada), pele de lobo/urso, crânio humano, totem, chapéu pontudo de mago, manto de monge com capuz, verde-oliva/floresta (Caçador), roupa violeta, monocromia marrom ou verde, camponês genérico, placa pesada. `` METAMORPH IDENTITY: a gentle mountain shepherd-healer who came back from the violet crystal with a crystal beast inside him; NOT a druid, NOT a monk, NOT a peasant. Unforgettable silhouette: a narrow body flaring into a wide bell-shaped triangular hem of layered felted wool, and a hooked shepherd's crook rising above the head. Fixed class headpiece on every level: a rounded felted lichen lime shepherd cap with two tied ear flaps and a small bell on its crown. Signature details: one big bellwether bell hanging on the chest from a thick leather collar strap (bigger and richer every tier), small bells along the hem, and the LEFT arm marked by matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 (violet only on the left arm, the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names, such as charms, flock beasts or sprouts; never on the cloth, never dominant). COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m, never a monochrome blur: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 wool is the dominant and brightest color, cream undyed wool #E8DFC8 as the light third tone on collars, cuffs and hem trims, peat brown #4A3B2A only as secondary on boots, belts, straps and under-layers, copper #B87333 on bells, pins and buckles; matte fabric, crisp color blocks. Forbidden: leaves, flowers, vines, antlers or any druid look, wolf or bear pelts and animal fur, human skulls, totems, pointed or wizard hats, hooded monk robe, forest green or olive (the Hunter's colors), violet or purple clothing, an all-brown or all-green monochrome outfit, generic peasant rags, heavy plate armor. ``

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1a. Conceito (UM conceito por nó, texto no singular; o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens do nó; pode ter arma): `` Concept art of ONE male Metamorph hero at level 1, a single full-body character, front three-quarter view, standing heroic and calm, holding his shepherd's crook. METAMORPH IDENTITY: a gentle mountain shepherd-healer who came back from the violet crystal with a crystal beast inside him; NOT a druid, NOT a monk, NOT a peasant. Unforgettable silhouette: a narrow body flaring into a wide bell-shaped triangular hem of layered felted wool, and a hooked shepherd's crook rising above the head. Fixed class headpiece on every level: a rounded felted lichen lime shepherd cap with two tied ear flaps and a small bell on its crown. Signature details: one big bellwether bell hanging on the chest from a thick leather collar strap (bigger and richer every tier), small bells along the hem, and the LEFT arm marked by matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 (violet only on the left arm, the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names, such as charms, flock beasts or sprouts; never on the cloth, never dominant). COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m, never a monochrome blur: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 wool is the dominant and brightest color, cream undyed wool #E8DFC8 as the light third tone on collars, cuffs and hem trims, peat brown #4A3B2A only as secondary on boots, belts, straps and under-layers, copper #B87333 on bells, pins and buckles; matte fabric, crisp color blocks. Forbidden: leaves, flowers, vines, antlers or any druid look, wolf or bear pelts and animal fur, human skulls, totems, pointed or wizard hats, hooded monk robe, forest green or olive (the Hunter's colors), violet or purple clothing, an all-brown or all-green monochrome outfit, generic peasant rags, heavy plate armor. LEVEL 1 OUTFIT: a rounded felted lichen lime shepherd cap with tied ear flaps and a small carved wooden bell on the crown; a knee-length lichen lime felted wool tunic flaring into a wide bell-shaped hem trimmed with a cream wool band and a row of tiny carved wooden bells; a short cream wool shoulder collar; a carved wooden bellwether bell on the chest hanging from a peat brown leather strap; a peat brown rope-and-leather belt; the whole left arm wrapped in rough cream bandages; soft peat brown wrapped boots with cream wool cuffs; simple peat leather wrist wraps; absolutely no metal yet (all bells are carved wood), no cape. Vivid lichen lime #A4C639 dominant, cream wool #E8DFC8, peat brown #4A3B2A, copper #B87333, matte, no glow. Hero quality of an AAA isometric action RPG (Lost Ark, Diablo IV level of character design): simple but unforgettable, clean readable shapes, stylized fantasy, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no glow. ` 1b. Pose T do escolhido (corpo-base masculino): ` Same Metamorph as the chosen concept, same face, same cap with tied ear flaps and wooden bell, same exact level 1 outfit with the wooden bellwether bell and the bandaged left arm, crook removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms wide open, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light, full body. ``

2. Corpo feminino base (a partir do piloto em pose T)

`` Same Metamorph as the input image turned into a woman: same cap with tied ear flaps and wooden bell, same level 1 outfit, palette, wooden bellwether bell and bandaged left arm, same height. REAL FEMALE CUT, never a slimmed-down man: a fitted tunic bodice shaped to her bust, a wide belt cinching a visibly narrow waist, the bell-shaped hem flaring from her hips like a skirt with full volume, long legs in high soft boots, graceful neck and shoulders, a soft feminine face; the volume lives in the cap, the cream collar and the flared hem. Fully covered, crook removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, arms wide open, palms down, empty hands, plain grey background, neutral light, full body. ``

3. Cabeças (peça separada; o gorro de lã fica em todas)

Cabeça a partir de um corpo: `` Extract only the head and neck of the character, then: the same head alone, front view, calm expression, wool shepherd cap kept, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, no shoulders, no staff, no glow. ` Masculinas (3): ` HEAD M1: Male Shepherd head, front view, short hair under a round lichen lime wool cap with one copper-colored lock at the temple, warm brown eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` ` HEAD M2: Male Shepherd head, front view, a thick braid coming out from under a lichen lime wool cap and hanging over one shoulder, short stubble, patient grey eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` ` HEAD M3: Male Shepherd head, front view, short curly hair under a lichen lime wool cap, a short shepherd's beard, kind green-brown eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` Femininas (3): ` HEAD F1: Female Shepherd head, front view, two low braids coming out from under a lichen lime wool cap, freckles, calm hazel eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` ` HEAD F2: Female Shepherd head, front view, short hair with a side fringe under a lichen lime wool cap, a peat brown ribbon, sharp dark eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` ` HEAD F3: Female Shepherd head, front view, long wavy hair tied low with a lime wool thread, a lichen lime wool cap pushed back, soft grey eyes, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, matte, no glow. ` Chifre de cristal (peça extra da S4 em diante; encaixa na têmpora esquerda de qualquer cabeça): ` Extract only the head, then: the same head with a small matte violet crystal horn growing from the left temple, pointing up and back, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, wool cap kept, plain grey background, neutral light, no body, no other glow. ``

4. Mãos

`` HAND M: Extract only the left hand of the male Shepherd, then: one open left hand alone, back view, matte violet crystal veins running along the back of the hand and fingers, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, rest of the hand normal skin, plain grey background, neutral light, no arm, no staff. ` ` HAND F: Extract only the left hand of the female Shepherd, then: one open left hand alone, back view, slender fingers, matte violet crystal veins running along the back of the hand and fingers, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, plain grey background, neutral light, no arm, no staff. ` ` HAND R: Extract only the right hand of the Shepherd, then: one open right hand alone, back view, a wrapped wool-and-leather wristband, plain grey background, neutral light, no arm, no staff, no glow. ``

5. Tabela de formas

Base Metamorfo (1) -> Pele-de-Cristal (20) -> A Guarda-Rebanho / B Fera-Brava (40) -> Pastor de Feras / Alfa de Cristal (60) -> Voz da Serra / Besta do Socalco (80) -> Pastor do Brotar / Colheita Brava (100) -> Rebanho Vivo / Fera Viva (130). Níveis com armadura: 1, 6, 11, 15, 21, 26, 31, 37 e, em A e B: 41, 44, 48, 51, 55, 60, 61, 65, 70, 71, 75, 80, 81, 85, 90, 91, 95, 100, 101, 105, 110, 111, 115, 120, 121, 125, 130, 131, 135, 140, 141, 145, 150. A = a fera é doença, domada (lã, sinos, rebanho de cristal; cristal coberto por manga de lã). B = a fera é dom (cristal à mostra no antebraço e na luva, garras, crista de lã). Cobre e metais só em pinos, sinos, ganchos e fivelas.

6. Prompts por nível

Regra de entrada: todo corpo (HOMEM, MULHER, HOMEM-A/B, MULHER-A/B) parte do corpo base em pose T, em todos os níveis; a ARMA parte da arma do nível anterior e é redesenhada. Passada de personalidade (19/11): todo corpo traz a IDENTIDADE nova; MULHER com FEMININE CUT (1-21) ou BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES, NOT IN HER BODY (26+); HOMEM com BULK do 26 em diante; sino-guia e cristal só crescem; 60 com VIVID ACCENTS + SHOWPIECE.

Nível 1 · S1 chave · Metamorfo (piloto)

Parte de: piloto aprovado (pose T). Marcas de longe: gorro de abas com sino de madeira, barra em sino com sininhos de madeira, sino-guia de madeira no peito, braço esquerdo enfaixado. `` HOMEM: Same Metamorph as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. METAMORPH IDENTITY: a gentle mountain shepherd-healer who came back from the violet crystal with a crystal beast inside him; NOT a druid, NOT a monk, NOT a peasant. Unforgettable silhouette: a narrow body flaring into a wide bell-shaped triangular hem of layered felted wool, and a hooked shepherd's crook rising above the head. Fixed class headpiece on every level: a rounded felted lichen lime shepherd cap with two tied ear flaps and a small bell on its crown. Signature details: one big bellwether bell hanging on the chest from a thick leather collar strap (bigger and richer every tier), small bells along the hem, and the LEFT arm marked by matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 (violet only on the left arm, the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names, such as charms, flock beasts or sprouts; never on the cloth, never dominant). FEMININE CUT: a fitted bodice shaped to her bust, the belt cinching a visibly narrow waist, the bell-shaped hem flaring from her hips like a skirt, long legs in high boots, volume in the cap, collar and hem pieces; a real woman's silhouette, never a slimmed-down man. Keep her feminine cut. ` ` ARMA: Same crook line as the input image, REDESIGNED for level 1: a NEW head and a NEW haft, plain curved wooden hook with a carved wooden bell hanging from its tip on a cord, two carved wooden rattles, a peat leather grip wrap and a small cream wool tassel. Sturdier and richer than the previous level: a thicker hook, more and bigger bells, wool and leather wraps. The crook alone, vertical, side view, full length, plain light grey background, neutral light, no hands, no glow. ``

Nível 6 · S1 intermediária · Lã Grossa

Parte de: corpo base em pose T. Marcas novas de longe: gola-rufo dupla creme e faixa listrada lima/creme no peito; barra em duas camadas. `` HOMEM: Same Metamorph as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 6 outfit. METAMORPH IDENTITY: a gentle mountain shepherd-healer who came back from the violet crystal with a crystal beast inside him; NOT a druid, NOT a monk, NOT a peasant. Unforgettable silhouette: a narrow body flaring into a wide bell-shaped triangular hem of layered felted wool, and a hooked shepherd's crook rising above the head. Fixed class headpiece on every level: a rounded felted lichen lime shepherd cap with two tied ear flaps and a small bell on its crown. Signature details: one big bellwether bell hanging on the chest from a thick leather collar strap (bigger and richer every tier), small bells along the hem, and the LEFT arm marked by matte violet crystal #8E5BD0 (violet only on the left arm, the temple horns and the small tamed crystal pieces the outfit names, such as charms, flock beasts or sprouts; never on the cloth, never dominant). COLOR CONTRAST, readable at 10 m, never a monochrome blur: vivid saturated lichen lime #A4C639 wool is the dominant and brightest color, cream undyed wool #E8DFC8 as the light third tone on collars, cuffs and hem trims, peat brown #4A3B2A only as secondary on boots, belts, straps and under-layers, copper #B87333 on bells, pins and buckles; matte fabric, crisp color blocks. Forbidden: leaves, flowers, vines, antlers or any druid look, wolf or bear pelts and animal fur, human skulls, totems, pointed or wizard hats, hooded monk robe, forest green or olive (the Hunter's colors), violet or purple clothing, an all-brown or all-green monochrome outfit, generic peasant rags, heavy plate armor. OUTFIT: the cap knitted thicker with longer ear flaps and a bigger carved wooden bell; a thick double tunic of felted lichen lime wool in two flared tiers, the lower tier edged in cream wool with wooden bell tassels; a big cream wool double ruff collar; a lime-and-cream striped wool sash across the chest; a bigger carved wooden bellwether bell on a braided strap; a wide peat tanned leather belt; the left arm bandaged to the elbow; NEW boots: tall peat hide boots wrapped crosswise with cream wool bands; NEW bracers: thick lime wool-wrapped cuffs on both forearms; still no metal, no cape. Vivid lichen lime #A4C639 dominant, cream wool #E8DFC8, peat brown #4A3B2A, copper #B87333, matte, no glow. ` ` MULHER: Same Metamorph as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE her current clothes and dress her in a COMPLETELY NEW level 6 outfit. OUTFIT: wool cap with a great crown of branching crystal horns and an ancient crown band; a colossal primordial beast-mantle of lichen lime wool to the ground with a spine of crystal and a pack's trophies lashed on it, small fossil skulls on it holding a subtle faint ember glow in the eye sockets; the crystal veins on the left claw gauntlet carry a very faint inner glow; a dark cinched ceremonial coat with engraved primordial plates; NEW skirt: ceremonial claw-point plates with trophy fangs; NEW boots: ceremonial primordial claw high boots; NEW bracers: a colossal left crystal claw gauntlet and a right ornate trophy bracer. Everything else matte; the only glow is the subtle ember in the skull eyes and the crystal veins. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet pale-blue crystal inlays with a faint soft inner glow, a quiet accent only, never dominant. Keep her feminine cut. ``

7. A FERA de cristal (modelo próprio, não humano)

Rig e animação: a Fera é quadrúpede: no Tripo usar o rig "Outro" (quadrúpede), não o preset Mixamo humanoide. Animações próprias (andar de quatro, galope, salto/Bote, patada, mordida, derrubada, transformação 0,6 s, ocioso); os clipes humanos do Mixamo não servem. Não é peça de armadura: um corpo à parte, um só até o nível 60; do 80 em diante há variantes A e B (decisão de Claude, conforme o ramo).
Identidade (GDD §3.7): baixa e comprida, de quatro, dorso arqueado, dois chifres de cristal para trás, ~1,1 m de altura no ombro; focinho curto; sem olhos brilhando; tufo de lã lima #A4C639 no dorso (a maior mancha vista de cima); cicatriz de cristal; corpo de cristal violeta escuro fosco. Nunca: asas, rabo longo, forma de lobo realista, folhas, totem. O cristal pode ter brilho interno leve (coerente com o Instinto no GDD); tudo o mais, fosco.

Conceito (4 livres): `` Four free concepts of the Crystal Beast of a mountain shepherd: a low and long four-legged beast of dark matte violet crystal #8E5BD0, arched back, two short crystal horns swept back, short blunt muzzle, plain dark eyes that do not glow, about 1.1 meters tall at the shoulder, a thick tuft of lichen lime #A4C639 wool along the back as the biggest patch seen from above, a crystal scar line across one shoulder, not a wolf, no wings, no long tail, a very faint inner glow inside the crystal only, stylized fantasy RPG, plain grey background, four different views. ` Pose neutra (a escolhida, para o 3D e o rig quadrúpede): ` Same Crystal Beast as the chosen concept, same design, standing neutral on all four legs with the legs slightly apart, head level, mouth closed, symmetric, strict side view, then a second front view, plain grey background, neutral light, full body, no shadow baked in, very faint inner glow inside the crystal only. `` Evolução visual (cada forma parte da Fera base em pose neutra; descreve o corpo inteiro do zero):

8. Arte de apresentação (nível 50 e nível 150)

Duas versões de cada peça: nível 50 e nível 150. Como não existe armadura de nível 50, o 50 usa o traje do nível 48 (o mais próximo); o 150 usa o traje de prestígio. Escolha um ramo (A ou B) e use o HOMEM e a MULHER aprovados desse ramo como imagens de entrada (mais a ARMA aprovada do nível).

KEY ART (homem e mulher juntos, 16:9, sem texto). Entrada: HOMEM + MULHER + ARMA do nível indicado.

Nível 50 (traje do nível 48): `` KEY ART 50: Using the input images as the exact characters, designs and outfits of level 48, a wide 16:9 cinematic key art of the male Metamorph and the female Metamorph standing side by side in a heroic three-quarter pose, each holding the shepherd's crook with bells from the weapon image, the matte violet crystal left forearm visible, the round wool shepherd cap with the small crystal horn clearly visible, the matte violet Crystal Beast with its lichen lime wool tuft standing calmly at their feet, in a misty terraced mountain slope of Ironroot at dawn, dramatic rim light, palette lichen lime #A4C639, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333, painterly stylized fantasy RPG key art, matte, no glow, no text, no logo, no frame. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. ` Nível 150: ` KEY ART 150: Using the input images as the exact characters, designs and outfits of level 150, a wide 16:9 cinematic key art of the male Metamorph and the female Metamorph standing side by side in a heroic three-quarter pose, each holding the shepherd's crook with bells from the weapon image, the matte violet crystal left forearm visible, the round wool shepherd cap with the small crystal horn clearly visible, the matte violet Crystal Beast with its lichen lime wool tuft standing calmly at their feet, in a misty terraced mountain slope of Ironroot at dawn, dramatic rim light, palette lichen lime #A4C639, peat brown #4A3B2A and copper #B87333, painterly stylized fantasy RPG key art, a very subtle faint inner glow only inside the crystal and the bells, no text, no logo, no frame. 9. Os 2 prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão)

Idênticos aos dos outros roteiros; nunca editar. Entrada: a imagem da ITEM (armadura) ou da ARMA aprovada do nível.

Fixo 1: ARMADURA (arte do item): `` The full torso armor from the input image displayed alone as a game item, including ALL its parts: collar or cape, both pauldrons, chest pieces and straps, belt and the full skirt. No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. Front view, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail. ``

Fixo 2: ARMA (arte do item): `` The same weapon from the input image displayed alone as a game item: identical design, materials and colors. Diagonal angle from bottom-left to top-right, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail, no hands, no text. ``
